			function footerSubscribe() {
				console.log('footerSubscribe #subscribe :'+$("#subscribeform #subscribe").val());
				var isValid = $('#subscribeform').valid();
				if (isValid) {
					//subscribe前再check login一次
					$.ajax({
						url : 'https://www.taiwantrade.com/login/checkLogin',
						type : "POST",
						async : true,
						data: {},
						dataType : 'text',
						contentType : "application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=utf-8",
						error : function(xhr, ajaxOptions, thrownError) {
							console.log(thrownError);
						}
					}).done(function(res) {
						//alert('You are already login, you will changed to subscribe by '+email);
						if(res != '') {
							var email = res;
							var oldemail = $("#subscribeform #subscribe").val();
							$("#subscribeform #subscribe").val(email);
							console.log('#subscribe :'+$("#subscribeform #subscribe").val());
							$("#subscribeform #subscribe").attr('readonly', true);
							$("#subscribeform #subscribe").attr('style','background: lightgray;');
							
							console.log('checkLogin :'+email+ 'vs'+ oldemail);
							if(oldemail!= '' && email!='' && oldemail.toLowerCase().trim() != email.toLowerCase().trim()){
								alert('You are already login, you will changed to subscribe by '+email);
								doSubscribe();
							}else doSubscribe();
						}else{
							$("#subscribeform #subscribe").attr('readonly', false);
							$("#subscribeform #subscribe").attr('style','background: none;');
							doSubscribe();
						}
					});						
					
				}
			}
			function doSubscribe() {
				$.ajax({
					url : 'https://www.taiwantrade.com/taiwantradeAlert/getEncodeEmail',
					type : "POST",
					async : true,
					data: {
						email: $("#subscribeform #subscribe").val()
					},
					dataType : 'text',
					contentType : "application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=utf-8",
					error : function(xhr, ajaxOptions, thrownError) {
						console.warn(thrownError);
					}
				}).done(function(data) {
					console.log('getEncodeEmail:'+data);
					//console.log('forward to :'+contextPath+'taiwantradeAlert/subscribeCategory/'+data)
					window.location.href = 'https://www.taiwantrade.com/taiwantradeAlert/subscribeCategory/'+data;
				});  			
			}
			function doWordKeyUp(obj,event){
				var id = obj.getAttribute('id');
				console.log('key up on #:'+id+' '+event.keyCode);
				if (event.keyCode == 13) {
					if(id == 'autocomplete'){
						doWordSubmit();
					}else if(id == 'subscribe'){
						footerSubscribe();
					}else{
						console.log('no action define for '+id+" in enter key up");
					}
				}
			}
						
			function doWordSubmit() {
			    var baseTarget = $('#btarget').attr('target'); 
				if(window.console) console.debug('baseTarget:'+baseTarget);
				if(baseTarget === undefined) baseTarget = '_self';
				if(window.console) console.debug('baseTarget:'+baseTarget);
				var searchWord = $('#autocomplete').val();
				if(window.console) console.debug('searchWord:'+searchWord);
				
				var selectItem = $('#searchform div.select_menu a.option_selected');
				if(window.console) console.debug('selectItem type:'+selectItem);
				if (searchWord == '') {
					// alert('Please Input Query Word');
				} else if (searchWord == '*') {
					 $('#autocomplete').val('');
				} else {
					if (selectItem.text() == 'Sample') {
						link = '/products/search/' + encodeURIComponent(searchWord) + '-gallery.html';
						window.open(link, baseTarget);
					} else if (selectItem.text() == 'Product') {
						link = '/products/search/' + encodeURIComponent(searchWord) + '-gallery.html';
						window.open(link, baseTarget);
					} else if (selectItem.text() == 'Supplier') {
						link = '/products/search/' + encodeURIComponent(searchWord) + '-supplier.html';
						window.open(link, baseTarget);
					} else if (selectItem.text() == 'Offer to Sell') {
						link = '/tradepost/search/' + encodeURIComponent(searchWord);
						window.open(link, baseTarget);
					}
				}
			}	
			function tryloginout(loginPrefix, target, redirectUrl) {
				// TCAP-4261: redirectUrl = currentUrl, 靜態化首頁的currentUrl會變成有加?previewHost=[首頁]，所以改用window.location.href
				if (typeof redirectUrl === "undefined" || redirectUrl == '') redirectUrl = window.location.href;
				if (typeof target === "undefined" || target == ''){ 
					target = "_self";
				}else{
					//if(window.console) console.log("top href:"+window.top.location.href);
					target = "_top";
					redirectUrl = window.top.location.href;
				}
				
				console.log('tryloginout for loginPrefix: '+loginPrefix);
				console.log('tryloginout for redirectUrl: '+redirectUrl);
				var loginUrl = loginPrefix + encodeURIComponent(redirectUrl)+"&locale=en".replace("-","_");
				
				var encodeStart = loginPrefix.indexOf("=http");
				if(encodeStart > 0){
					var firstLoginPrefix = loginPrefix.substring(0,encodeStart+1);
					var secondLoginUrl = loginPrefix.substring(encodeStart+1);
					console.log('tryloginout for secondLoginUrl: '+secondLoginUrl);
					var secondUrl = secondLoginUrl + encodeURIComponent(redirectUrl);
					if(window.console) console.log("secondUrl:"+secondUrl);
					loginUrl = firstLoginPrefix + encodeURIComponent(secondUrl)+"&locale=en";
				}
				
				if(window.console) console.log("loginUrl:"+loginUrl);
				if(window.console) console.log("target:"+target);
				window.open(loginUrl,target)
			}
			function getSsodomain() {
				if (location.hostname.endsWith("develop.ttstaging.com.tw")){return "sso.develop.ttstaging.com.tw"}
				else if (location.hostname === "localhost"){return "sso.develop.ttstaging.com.tw"}
				else if (location.hostname.endsWith("ttstaging.com.tw")){return "sso.ttstaging.com.tw"}
				else if (location.hostname.endsWith("develop.ttstaging.com.tw")){return "sso.develop.ttstaging.com.tw"}
				else if (location.hostname.endsWith("ttstaging.com.tw")){return "sso.ttstaging.com.tw"}
				else return "security.taiwantrade.com";
			}

			function push(method){
				if(alreadyDOMContentLoaded){
					method();
				} else {
					methods.push(method);
				}
			}
			
			document.addEventListener( 'DOMContentLoaded', function () {

				methods.forEach(function(item, index){
				    try {
        					item();
				    } catch(e) {
				        console.log(e);
				    }
				});
			}, false );

			function clickItem(clickType) {
				if ($(window).width() <= 768) {
					if (clickType == 'service') {
						showBlock('ulService', 'hrefService');
						hideBlock('ulTaiwantrade', 'hrefTaiwantrade');
						hideBlock('ulLanguage', 'hrefLanguage');
					} else if (clickType == 'taiwantrade') {
						hideBlock('ulService', 'hrefService');
						showBlock('ulTaiwantrade', 'hrefTaiwantrade');
						hideBlock('ulLanguage', 'hrefLanguage');
					} else if (clickType == 'language') {
						hideBlock('ulService', 'hrefService');
						hideBlock('ulTaiwantrade', 'hrefTaiwantrade');
						showBlock('ulLanguage', 'hrefLanguage');
					}
				}
			}
			
			function showBlock(ulName, hrefName) {
				try {
					var ulPart = document.getElementById(ulName);
					var hrefPart = document.getElementById(hrefName);

					if (ulPart.style.display == '' || ulPart.style.display == 'none') {
						hrefPart.className = 'stBtn hover';
						ulPart.style.display = 'block';
					} else if (ulPart.style.display == 'block') {
						hrefPart.className = 'stBtn';
						ulPart.style.display = 'none';
					}
				} catch (e) {
				}
			}

			function hideBlock(ulName, hrefName) {
				try {
					var ulPart = document.getElementById(ulName);
					var hrefPart = document.getElementById(hrefName);

					hrefPart.className = 'stBtn';
					ulPart.style.display = 'none';
				} catch (e) {
				}
			}

				function adjustHeights(){
					redirectForMobileOrNot('true','');
					adjustHeight('div.four-products div.column_column h2');
					//adjustHeight('div.four-products div.column_slider.one');
					adjustHeight('div.eva-info div.column_attr h2');
					adjustImgHeightAsDivWeight('div.entry-content #blockRV div.column_zoom_box','div.entry-content #blockRV div.column_zoom_box img.scale-with-grid');
					adjustImgHeightAsDivWeight('div.four-products div.column.mcb-column.one.column_slider','div.four-products div.column.mcb-column.one.column_slider img.scale-with-grid');
				}
				function adjustImgHeightAsDivWeight(divClass,imgClass){
					var divs = $(divClass);
					console.debug('find '+divs.length+' div $('+divClass+')');
    				var highest = 0;
    				if(divs.length > 0) {
						divs.each(function( index ) {
							console.debug( 'find '+index + ': ' + $(this).width() + ' x ' + $(this).height() +' | ' + $( this ).text()  );
							if($(this).width() > highest) highest = $(this).width();
						});
	    				console.log( 'get max width:'+ highest+' between '+divs.length+' div $('+divClass+')' );
	    				var imgs = $(imgClass);
	    				if(highest > 1){
		    				imgs.height(highest);
		    				console.log('set height:'+highest+'px !important'+' to '+imgs.length+' imgs $('+imgClass+')');
		    			}
	    			}else{
	    				console.log( 'no elements for div $('+divClass+')' );
	    			}					
				}
				function adjustHeight(divClass){
					var divs = $(divClass);
					console.debug('find '+divs.length+' div $('+divClass+')');
    				var highest = 0;
    				if(divs.length > 0) {
						divs.each(function( index ) {
							console.debug( 'find '+index + ': ' + $(this).width() + ' x ' + $(this).height() +' | ' + $( this ).text()  );
							if($(this).height() > highest) highest = $(this).height();
						});
	    				console.log( 'get max height:'+ highest+' between '+divs.length+' div $('+divClass+')' );
	    				if(highest > 1) divs.height(highest);
	    			}else{
	    				console.log( 'no elements for div $('+divClass+')' );
	    			}
				}				
				function loadSPBlock(blockName,divId,blockIds) {
					var url = '/HOME/sp/' + blockName + '?isMobile=true&did=2';
					
					$.post(url, 
						function(blockHtml) {
							$('#'+divId).html(blockHtml);
							$.each(blockIds,function(index,blockId){
								var frName = '#'+divId+' #'+blockId+' [id^='+blockId+'_]';
								var toName = '#Wrapper #'+blockId+' [id^='+blockId+'_]';
								console.log('@@ replace all li from '+frName+'('+$(frName).length+') '+toName+'('+$(toName).length+') @@');
								$(frName).each(function(){
									var li_id = $(this).attr('id');
									var sourceli = $(this);
									var targetli = $(toName+'#'+li_id);
									console.debug('@  try replace '+targetli.length+' of '+toName+'#'+$(targetli).attr('id')+'.'+targetli.attr('class')+ 'target from: '+frName+'#'+$(this).attr('id')+'.'+$(this).attr('class'));
									targetli.each(function(){
										var frHtml = sourceli.html().replace(/>\s+</g,'><').trim();
										var toHtml = $(this).html().replace(/>\s+</g,'><').trim();
										if(toHtml != frHtml){
											console.debug($(this).attr('id')+'->');
											console.debug('targetli from: '+frName+'#'+sourceli.attr('id')+'.'+sourceli.attr('class')+':');
											console.debug('         to  : '+toName+'#'+$(this).attr('id')+'.'+$(this).attr('class')+':');
											console.debug('targetli from: '+frHtml);
											console.debug('         to  : '+toHtml);
											//if($(this).attr('id') == 'blockKV_1') alert('reload '+$(this).attr('id'));
											$(this).fadeOut(500,function(){
												$(this).html(sourceli.html()).fadeIn(500, function(){
													$(this).addClass('done');
													lazyLoadImg(toName+'#'+$(this).attr('id')+' img.lazy');
													console.debug('->  targetli done: '+toName+'#'+$(this).attr('id')+'.'+$(this).attr('class'));//+':'+$(this).html());
													if(blockName == 'SP_RECENTVIEW'){
														adjustImgHeightAsDivWeight('div.entry-content #blockRV div.column_zoom_box','div.entry-content #blockRV div.column_zoom_box img.scale-with-grid');
													}
												});
											});
										}else{
											console.debug('|>  no need to replace '+toName+'#'+$(this).attr('id')+'.'+$(this).attr('class')+' from '+frName+'#'+sourceli.attr('id')+'.'+sourceli.attr('class'));
										}
									
									});
									
								});
							});
						}
					)
					.fail(function (jqXHR, textStatus) {
						console.debug('fail load '+blockName+' at #'+divId+' blocks:'+blockIds+'['+textStatus+':'+jqXHR+']');
						$.each(blockIds,function(index,blockId){
							var toName = '#Wrapper #'+blockId+' [id^='+blockId+'_]';
							console.log('lazyLoadImg when ws fail '+toName+'('+$(toName).length+') @@');
							lazyLoadImg(toName+' img.lazy');
							if(blockName == 'SP_RECENTVIEW'){
								adjustImgHeightAsDivWeight('div.entry-content #blockRV div.column_zoom_box','div.entry-content #blockRV div.column_zoom_box img.scale-with-grid');
							}
						});
					})
					.always(function() {
					});
				}
				
				function lazyLoadImg(imgIds){
					try{
						var placeholderImg = "//cs01.itaiwantrade.com/front/f088260/tteng/images/public/360x360.png";
						if($(imgIds).length > 0){
							lazyLoadCount+=$(imgIds).length;
							$(imgIds).error(function () {
								console.warn("imgLazyLoad error for $("+imgIds+")| "+ $(imgIds).length +" imgs");
				           	});
							if(window.console) console.info("start imgLazyLoad for $("+imgIds+") |id="+$(imgIds).attr('id')+",data-original="+$(imgIds).attr('data-original')+"| "+ $(imgIds).length +" imgs, rest lazyLoadCount:"+lazyLoadCount);
							$(imgIds).lazyload({
								event : "sporty",
								threshold : 10000,
								effect : "fadeIn",
								skip_invisible : false,
								placeholder :	placeholderImg,
								load : function(elements_left,  settings) {
									lazyLoadCount --;
									console.log($(this).attr('src') + ' loaded, rest lazyLoadCount:'+lazyLoadCount);
								}
							});
							// for lazy  load
							$('body,html').animate({scrollTop:1},1);
						}else{
							if(window.console) console.info("imgLazyLoad nothing for $("+imgIds+") because "+ $(imgIds).length +" imgs");
						}
						
					} catch (err) {
						console.warn(err);
					}				
				}

				$('#searchform').keypress(function (event) {            
					if (event.keyCode == 13) {
						if ($('#autocomplete').val() != '') {
							var selectItem = $('#searchform div.select_menu a.option_selected');
							var searchWord = $('#autocomplete').val();
							if (selectItem.text() == 'Sample') {
								link = '/products/search/' + encodeURIComponent(searchWord) + '-gallery.html';
								window.location.href = link;
							} else if (selectItem.text() == 'Product') {
								link = '/products/search/' + encodeURIComponent(searchWord) + '-gallery.html';
								console.log('here::' + link);
								window.location.href = link;
							} else if (selectItem.text() == 'Supplier') {
								link = '/products/search/' + encodeURIComponent(searchWord) + '-supplier.html';
								window.location.href = link;
							} else if (selectItem.text() == 'Offer to Sell') {
								link = '/tradepost/search/' + encodeURIComponent(searchWord);
								window.location.href = link;
							}
						}
					}
				});

			});
			
			//產品搜尋框輔助
			methods.push(searchBarSearchSuggestionsMethod);
			function searchBarSearchSuggestionsMethod(){
				var selectItem = $('#searchform div.select_menu a.option_selected');
				console.log('searchBarSearchSuggestionsMethod at :'+selectItem.text());
				if (selectItem.text() == 'Product') {
					bindAutoComplete();
				}
	
				selectItem.click(function(){
					if (selectItem.text() == 'Product') {
						$('#autocomplete').autoComplete("destroy");
						bindAutoComplete();
					} else if (selectItem.text() == 'Supplier') {
						$('#autocomplete').autoComplete("destroy");
					}
				});
	
			}			
			function bindAutoComplete() {
				console.log('bindAutoComplete:');
	            $('#autocomplete').autoComplete({ 
	            	appendTo: '#autocomplete',
	                minChars: 1,
	                source: function(term, suggest){
	                	console.log('autoComplete:'+term.toLowerCase());
	                    term = term.toLowerCase();
						$.ajax({
							url : contextPath+'searchSuggestions',
							type : "POST",
							contentType : "application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=utf-8",
							dataType : "json",
							data:{
								did: $('#did').val(), 
								keyword: term,
							},
							error : function(xhr, ajaxOptions, thrownError) {
								console.log(thrownError);
							}
						}).done(function(data) {
							suggest(data);
						});	 
	                },
	                renderItem: function (item, search){
	                    var re = new RegExp("(" + search.split(' ').join('|') + ")", "gi");
	                    var showWord = '<div class="autocomplete-suggestion" data-keyword="'+item.word+'" data-catalog="'+item.cid+'" data-val="'+item.word+'">';              
	                    var showWordBold = item.word.replace(re, "<b>$1</b>");
	                    var showData = "";
	                    var catalogTag = "";
	                    if (item.rowNum == 1) {
	                    	showData += '<p class="autocomplete-title" >Search Suggestions</p>';
	                    }
	                    if (item.isTopKeyword && item.rowNum != 1) {
	                    	showWord += '<hr class="autocomplete-hr" />';
	                    }
						// 產業top                    
	                    if (item.csName) { 
	                    	catalogTag = '<span> in </span><span style="color:red">'+item.csName+'</span>'
	                    }
	                    showData = showData + showWord + showWordBold + catalogTag +'</div>'
	                    console.log('renderItem:'+item.word+' with showData:'+showData);
	                    return showData;
	                },
	                onSelect: function(e, term, item){
	                	console.log('onSelect:'+term.toLowerCase());
	                	var keyword = "*";
	                	if ($.trim(item.data("keyword")) !='') {
	                		keyword = item.data("keyword");
	                	}
	                	if (item.data("catalog")) {
	                	    window.location.href=contextPath+'products/search?word='+keyword+'&cate_standard='+item.data("catalog")+'&type=product';
	                	} else {
	                		$('#autocomplete').val(item.data('keyword'));
	                   		doWordSubmit();
	                	}
	                }
	        	});
	        	$('#autocomplete').focus(function(){
	        		$('#autocomplete').trigger('keyup.autocomplete'); 
	        	});
			}			
			methods.push(headerFrameOnloadCallBack);
			function headerFrameOnloadCallBack() {
				if (headerFrameLoaded) {
					if(window.console) console.debug("headerFrameLoaded");
					try{
						if($('#frame1').contents().length > 0){
							var c = $('#frame1').contents().find("body").html();
							if (c.indexOf('div class="TopBar"') == 1 && c.indexOf('div class="TopNav" id="show_right"') > 0) {
								if(window.console) console.debug("add TopBar to show_right");
								var d = c.substring(60, c.length - 13);
								$('#show_right').html(d);
								if(window.console) console.debug("show_right:"+$('#show_right').html());
								var welcome_li = $('#show_right').find('#welcome_li');
								$('div.secondary_menu_wrapper.eva_pc #menu-quick-contact').prepend('<li class="current_page_item"><span>'+welcome_li.text()+'</span></li>');
								$('a.eva_sign.eva_mb').empty();
								$('a.eva_sign.eva_mb').prepend(welcome_li.text()+'<i class="icon-user"></i>');
								
								$('div.secondary_menu_wrapper.eva_pc #menu-quick-contact #sourcing-service').empty();
								$('div.search_wrapper.test02 #menu-quick-contact #m-sourcing-service').empty();
								
								var myTaiwantrade = $('#show_right #hrefTaiwantrade ~ ul li');
								console.debug('myTaiwanTrade:'+$('#show_right #hrefTaiwantrade').text());
								console.debug('sourcing service:'+$('div.search_wrapper.test02 #menu-quick-contact #m-sourcing-service').prev('a').find('span').text());
								$('#menu-quick-contact #sourcing-service').prev('span').html($('#show_right #hrefTaiwantrade').text()+'<a class="icon-down-open"></a>');
								$('div.search_wrapper.test02 #menu-quick-contact #m-sourcing-service').prev('a').find('span').html($('#show_right #hrefTaiwantrade').text()+'<a class="icon-down-open"></a>');
								myTaiwantrade.each(function(i){
									var node = $(this).clone();
									node.attr('class',i== myTaiwantrade.length - 1 ? 'submenu last-item' : 'submenu');
									$('#menu-quick-contact #sourcing-service').append(node);
									var mnode = $(this).clone();
									mnode.attr('class',i== myTaiwantrade.length - 1 ? 'menu-item menu-item-type-post_type menu-item-object-page last-item' : 'menu-item menu-item-type-post_type menu-item-object-page');
									$('div.search_wrapper.test02 #menu-quick-contact #m-sourcing-service').append(mnode);
								});
								$('div.secondary_menu_wrapper.eva_pc #menu-quick-contact li.watchlist a').attr('href', $('#show_right #favorites').attr('href'));
								$('div.search_wrapper.test02 #menu-quick-contact li.watchlist a').attr('href', $('#show_right #favorites').attr('href'));
								
								var signOutNode = $('#show_right #signOutNode');
								
								$('div.search_wrapper.test02 #menu-quick-contact #m_register').empty();
								$('div.search_wrapper.test02 #menu-quick-contact #m_register').append(signOutNode.clone());
								$('div.secondary_menu_wrapper.eva_pc #menu-quick-contact #sigin').empty();
								$('div.secondary_menu_wrapper.eva_pc #menu-quick-contact #sigin').append(signOutNode.clone());
								$('div.secondary_menu_wrapper.eva_pc #menu-quick-contact #register').remove();
							} else {
								if(window.console) console.debug("find no header for show_right");
								//$('#show_right').html(c);
							}
						}
					} catch (err) {
						console.warn(err);
					}

					initHeaderFavorites();
				} else {
					setTimeout(function() { headerFrameOnloadCallBack() }, 50);
				}
			}
			function initHeaderFavorites() {
				try {
					$.fn.favorites.headers();
				} catch (err) {
					if(window.console) console.warn('fail to initHeaderFavorites because:'+err);
				}
			}
			function goToFavorite(){
               $.ajax({
                   url : '/favorites/goToFavorites',
                   type : "POST",
                   data: {
                       favoriteType: 'PRODUCT'
                   },
                   success : function(json) {
                       window.location = json;
                   }
               });
            }
			function clickItem(clickType) {
				var mobileVersion = 'true';
				if ($(window).width() <= 768 || mobileVersion == 'true') {
					if (clickType == 'service') {
						showBlock('sourcing-service', 'hrefService');
						hideBlock('sourcing-service_none', 'hrefTaiwantrade');
						hideBlock('language-service', 'hrefLanguage');
					} else if (clickType == 'taiwantrade') {
						hideBlock('sourcing-service_none', 'hrefService');
						showBlock('sourcing-service', 'hrefTaiwantrade');
						hideBlock('language-service', 'hrefLanguage');
					} else if (clickType == 'language') {
						hideBlock('sourcing-service', 'hrefService');
						hideBlock('sourcing-service', 'hrefTaiwantrade');
						showBlock('language-service', 'hrefLanguage');
					}
				}
			}
			
			function showBlock(ulName, hrefName) {
				try {
					var ulPart = document.getElementById(ulName);
					var hrefPart = document.getElementById(hrefName);

					if (ulPart.style.display == '' || ulPart.style.display == 'none') {
						hrefPart.className = 'stBtn hover';
						ulPart.style.display = 'block';
					} else if (ulPart.style.display == 'block') {
						hrefPart.className = 'stBtn';
						ulPart.style.display = 'none';
					}
				} catch (e) {
				}
			}

			function hideBlock(ulName, hrefName) {
				try {
					var ulPart = document.getElementById(ulName);
					var hrefPart = document.getElementById(hrefName);

					hrefPart.className = 'stBtn';
					ulPart.style.display = 'none';

			function open_close(id) {
				var o = document.getElementById(id);
				if (id == 'show_left') {
					var or = document.getElementById('show_right');
					if (or != null) {
						or.style.display = 'none';
					}
				}

				if (id == 'show_right') {
					var ol = document.getElementById('show_left');
					if (ol != null) {
						ol.style.display = 'none';
					}
				}

				if (o != null) {
					if (o.style.display == 'block') {
						o.style.display = 'none';
					} else {
						o.style.display = 'block';
					}
				}
			}
